# ZS-M68 — Holonomy creates the frame, erases every transverse-resource record, and conserves the light-cone population

### Reference-state return dynamics of a reversible trace-three holonomy on a chiral-bag boundary carrier: the Bloch rapidity law, transverse depletion with sharp `N`-return ceilings, the canonical two-outcome instrument (reference-resource decay law at `φ⁻⁴`), the coherent-reference record transducer with its amplification bound and faithfulness identity, the survival classification of Clifford-pair records by the light-cone projection `w_K` of the resource axis, the convergence of actual fresh-copy collisions to the limiting record, the no-unital-refresh theorems, and the chiral-bag antiunitary lemma

> **One-line scope verdict (v1.4).** Under the declared reversible trace-three holonomy `(A,J)` and the ZS-M67 boundary-carrier
> assumptions, we derive the exact return dynamics of a two-level reference state — each post-selected return is a Lorentz boost of
> rapidity `2η = 2 arcosh(3/2)` on its Stokes vector, and the canonical two-outcome completion of the post-selection has an
> unconditional channel that is exact K-dephasing at `φ⁻⁴` (the **reference-resource decay law**, Thm 4.6); passive return
> drives both transverse components `(m_{K'}, m_J)` to zero — exactly `m_J(0)cosh ζ₀/cosh(ζ₀+2nη)` conditionally, hence bounded by
> `sech(ζ₀+2nη)`, and exactly `φ^{−4n}m_J(0)` unconditionally — with sharp `N`-return ceilings `sech((N−1)η)` (`2/3`, `2/7`), while the
> longitudinal light-cone population `m_K` is conserved (unconditionally) or saturated (conditionally). For the Clifford-pair
> couplings `X_E⊗(u·σ) + χY_E⊗(v·σ)` the collision fixed point is unique for `sin 2α ≠ 0` with record `κ_* = −χ m_w g(cos2α, T_w²)`,
> `g = (1−c)/(1−(1−T_w²)c)`, for the reference component `m_w` along `w = u×v`, with the non-strict bound `|κ_*| ≤ 2|m_w|/(2−T_w²)
> ≤ 2|m_w|/(1+m_w²)` (equality conditions exact) and the exact faithfulness identity. **Survival is decided by the light-cone
> projection `w_K = w·e_K` of the resource axis, per reading:** every coupling with `w_K = 0` — a class that *contains* the
> joint-grading-even couplings `w = ±e_J` as a proper subset — has vanishing limiting record under passive return in both readings,
> and no unital refresh restores it unconditionally (Thms 6.5–6.6). *Conditionally* (`m_K(0) > −1`) the limiting record
> `κ_∞ = −χ w_K(1−cos2α)/(1−w_K²cos2α)` vanishes iff `w_K = 0`, is biased and faithful iff `0 < |w_K| < 1`, and is a pure
> (non-faithful) pole at `|w_K| = 1`. *Unconditionally* the limiting record `−χ w_K m_K(0)(1−cos2α)/D_w` vanishes iff `w_K m_K(0) = 0` and is biased and faithful
> iff `0 < |w_K m_K(0)| < 1` — so `m_K(0) = 0` gives no bias for any axis, while `|w_K| = 1` with `0 < |m_K(0)| < 1` does give a
> faithful biased record `κ_∞ = −χ w_K m_K(0)` without refresh. In particular the **A-derived longitudinal references** `A^q/tr A^q = ½(I + tanh(qη)K)`,
> which are unconditionally return-invariant, read by a resource axis `w = e_K` (itself `(2A−3I)/√5`) give the faithful biased record `−χ tanh(qη)`
> with margin `sech²(qη)` at every admissible angle (Cor 5.12): the reversal-blindness lemma `Tr f(A)J = 0` excludes a J-population of a function of `A`,
> **not** a longitudinal resource (v1.3 §6.7 said otherwise; withdrawn).
> The actual sequential fresh-copy collision process reaches these limiting records (Lemma 5.10; carrier relaxation is Schur-stable
> for every reference). The carrier record's lifetime is governed by the collision relaxation as well as by the reference decay:
> `φ^{−4n}` is the reference law, not the carrier law (Prop 5.11). Comparison with the Z-Spin locked inputs is made only after
> freezing: a `T₂`-magnitude capacity survives at most two passive returns in the transverse-resource class — and two returns are
> attained at a finite admissible angle (`16/19 > T₂` on consecutive points of the pure orbit, faithful) — and in the conditional
> tilted model a `T₂`-magnitude record is attained faithfully at some admissible angle iff `x(T₂) < |w_K| < 1`, `x(T₂) = 0.53907012…`
> (strict and exact: `|w_K| = x(T₂)` gives only a supremum at the excluded angle `α = π/2`; the rounded `0.5391` is not the boundary).
> **For the soldered resource-axis class `w = ±e_J`** (where the transducer coherence `T_w` is the M60 odd coherence `T`) the K10 joint demand (`T ≥ M_*` and `|κ| = T₂` on one reference)
> is excluded at a fixed angle iff `cos 2α > c_crit = −0.6369…`; in the **general** Clifford-pair class it is not excluded — `w = e_K`, `m = T₂e_K`, `α = π/4` attains `|κ_*| = T₂` faithfully
> with `T = T₂ > M_*` (W66; `T_w ≠ T` off the soldered class). Separately, a `k_⊥`-dressed antiunitary symmetry of the chiral-bag
> half-space Dirac operator forces the pointwise level-wise vanishing of the axial density. **Nothing here is a selection**: which
> resource axis, collision angle, chirality, initial population, instrument strength, failure branch and reference supply the action
> provides are open (§6.7); the subsystem/dilation structure behind the reduced collision channel (A5) is itself a hypothesis; no S14 derivation, definite outcome, Born weighting or physical clock is claimed.

## 0. 한국어 요약

**질문.** 가역 정수 holonomy `(A,J)`, `JAJ=A⁻¹`, `tr A = 3`을 ZS-M67의 chiral-bag 경계 carrier에 solder했을 때, 그 carrier가 물리적 기록을 **만들면서 동시에 보존**할 수 있는가.

**답(이 논문의 범위 안에서; v1.2에서 분류가 정정됨).** *만들기:* 예. Clifford-pair 결합 `X_E⊗(u·σ)+χY_E⊗(v·σ)`의 collision 채널은 `sin2α≠0`이면 유일한 고정점을 가지며, 그 기록값은 기준상태의 **자원축 성분** `m_w = m·w`, `w=u×v`에 비례한다: `κ_* = −χ m_w (1−cos2α)/(1−(1−T_w²)cos2α)`(Thm 5.2, 5.8; 열적 대각 ancilla의 경우는 문헌 결과의 특수화). 정확한 상한은 **비엄격** `|κ_*| ≤ 2|m_w|/(2−T_w²) ≤ 2|m_w|/(1+m_w²) ≤ 1`이며 첫 부등식은 `m_w≠0`, `T_w>0`일 때만 엄격하고(Thm 5.3), 고정점은 순수 대각 기준상태 `(T_w=0, |m_w|=1)`를 제외하면 faithful하다(Thm 5.7, 정확 항등식). *보존하기:* **자원축의 광추 투영 `w_K = w·e_K` 하나가 생존을 결정한다.** return은 기준상태의 Stokes 4-벡터에 rapidity `2η = 2 arcosh(3/2)`의 로렌츠 boost로 작용하고(Thm 4.3), 그 canonical 두-결과 instrument의 무조건 채널은 K-기저 dephasing이며 인자는 정확히 `φ⁻⁴`다(Thm 4.6 — **기준계 자원 감소 법칙**). 따라서 횡성분 `(m_{K'}, m_J)`는 조건부로 `m_J(0)cosh ζ₀/cosh(ζ₀+2nη)`(상한 `sech(ζ₀+2nη)`), 무조건부로 정확히 `φ^{−4n}m_J(0)`로 소멸하고(Thm 4.4, 4.6; `N`번 연속 유지 상한 `sech((N−1)η)` = `2/3`, `2/7`, Thm 4.5), 종성분 `m_K`는 보존(무조건)·포화(조건부)된다.

**정정된 생존 분류(Thm 5.9; v1.3에서 reading별로 다시 명시).** `w_K = 0`인 모든 결합은 두 reading 모두에서 극한 기록이 0이다 — 이 부류는 joint-grading-even 결합(`w=±e_J`)을 **진부분집합**으로 포함한다(반례 `(e_K,−e_J,e_{K'})`, W45). 대칭 분류는 `[H_R, J_E⊗J]=0 ⇔ w=±e_J`(Thm 5.8(b))이다. **조건부**(`m_K(0)>−1`): `κ_∞ = −χ w_K(1−cos2α)/(1−w_K²cos2α)`는 `w_K=0`일 때에만 0이고, **biased이면서 faithful ⇔ `0<|w_K|<1`**, `|w_K|=1`은 순수 극점. **무조건부**: 극한 기록은 **`w_K m_K(0)=0`일 때에만 0**(`w_K=0`만이 아니다: `w_K=3/5`, `m_K(0)=0`도 0), **biased이면서 faithful ⇔ `0<|w_K m_K(0)|<1`** — 따라서 `|w_K|=1`이라도 `0<|m_K(0)|<1`이면 refresh 없는 faithful biased 기록이 된다(`κ_∞=−χ w_K m_K(0)`, 즉 `w=+e_K`이면 `−χ m_K(0)`, `w=−e_K`이면 `+χ m_K(0)`; 여유 `1−m_K(0)²`; C60. v1.3은 부호 `w_K`를 빠뜨리고 `κ_∞=−χ m_K(0)`로 적었으며 이를 정정한다 — v1.3 감사 F3). v1.2의 요약·claim map·결론은 이 두 reading을 섞어 "`w_K=0` iff"와 "`0<|w_K|<1` 필요"로 썼으며 **철회**한다(v1.2 감사 F1). 목표값 `ρ`는 **조건부 모형에서**, 그리고 **`0<|w_K|<1`일 때에만**, `sup_α|κ_∞| = 2|w_K|/(1+w_K²)`가 달성되지 않는 supremum이므로(끝점 `|w_K|∈{0,1}`에서는 모든 허용각에서 각각 `0`, `1`로 달성된다 — C68, v1.3 감사 F4) 허용 각에서의 실제 도달은 엄격 `|w_K| > x(ρ)`, faithful까지는 `x(ρ)<|w_K|<1`; `ρ=T₂`이면 정확히 `x(T₂)=0.5390701239…`이며 반올림값 `0.5391`은 경계가 아니다(`a=0.53908`, `cos2α=−0.99999`에서 `κ>T₂`; W64). 극한 고정점이 실제 순차 충돌의 극한임은 **Lemma 5.10**(감사자 유도, 여기서 증명: 모든 기준상태에서 carrier 완화 행렬은 Schur-stable, 특성다항식 `(λ−c)[λ²−c(1+c)λ+d]`)으로 확립된다 — 단, 매 충돌마다 독립 새 reference copy를 공급하는 A5 모형 안에서만.

**두 return의 양의 증인(Cor 5.4(iv), 감사 보강).** 순수 궤도의 연속 두 점 `m^{(0)}=(−√5/3,0,2/3)`, `m^{(1)}=(+√5/3,0,2/3)`(한 번의 수동 return)은 유한 허용각 `cos2α=−3/5`에서 held-reference capacity가 정확히 `16/19=0.8421>T₂`이고 faithful(여유 `625/3249`)이다(C62): "최대 두 번"의 두 번은 supremum이 아니라 유한 각에서 실제로 달성된다 — 단 capacity 증인이며 carrier 수명이 아니다. **K10(Cor 7.1) 정정과 범위(v1.4):** **soldered 자원축 부류 `w=±e_J`에서만**(이 부류에서만 transducer coherence `T_w`가 M60의 odd coherence `T`와 같다) 고정 각의 최대 capacity는 envelope `B(c)=m_max(1−c)/(1−m_max²c)`, `m_max=√(1−M_*²)`이고 배제는 **`c>c_crit=−0.6369…`일 때에만(iff)**; `cos2α≥0`은 충분조건일 뿐이다(`c=−1/10`에서도 `B=0.6821<T₂`; C61). v1.2의 "kill iff `cos2α≥0`"은 철회한다. **일반 Clifford-pair 부류에는 이 배제를 적용할 수 없다:** `w=e_K`, `m=T₂e_K`, `α=π/4`이면 `T=T₂>M_*`, `T_w=0`, `M=0`, `κ_*=−T₂`(faithful, 여유 `1−T₂²`)로 `c=0>c_crit`에서도 joint demand가 **달성**된다(W66; v1.3 감사 F2). 일반화하려면 `T_w≥M_*`라는 **다른 가정**을 새로 선언해야 하며 M60의 `T` 하한에서 자동 수입할 수 없다. **Schur 안정성 ≠ 일회 엄격수축:** `m=e_K`, `α=π/4`에서 `M`은 멱영이지만 `‖M‖₂=1`이고 한 번의 충돌에서 trace distance 1이 보존된다(W59); Lemma 5.10(b)는 adapted norm으로 증명되므로 유지되나 v1.2 §10.3-7의 "엄격 수축 확보"는 철회한다.

**수명의 분리(Prop 5.11).** `φ^{−4n}`은 **기준계** 자원의 감소율이다. carrier 기록 `κ_n`은 충돌 완화율 `c²=cos²2α`와 기준계 감소율 `f=φ⁻⁴`가 함께 지배한다: K-무편극 기준상태에서 `κ_{n+1} = c²κ_n − χ(1−c²)m₀fⁿ`, 정확한 4×4 반례 `α=π/6`, `m₀=1/2`: `κ₁=−3/8`, `κ₂=(−45+18√5)/32`, `κ₂−fκ₁=−3/32≠0`(C50). v1.1의 "무조건 선형 법칙이 carrier 기록의 수명을 준다"는 **철회**한다. 또 canonical instrument의 실패 분기에는 횡 기대값이 저장되지 않는다(`Tr(J E₁σE₁)=0`; v1.1의 "실패 분기로 희석" 표현 철회).

**refresh no-go(unital 부류 한정).** 무조건 채널 뒤(앞)에 어떤 unital 연산을 두어도 횡성분이 0이 아닌 고정점은 없고(Thm 6.5), refresh가 매 cycle 달라져도 횡자원은 점근적으로 소멸한다: `(1−f²)Σ|P_⊥r_n|² ≤ |r₀|²`(**Thm 6.6**, 감사자 유도). 감사의 involutive cycle `𝒞=Ad_J∘ℋ`는 `𝒞²=id`, 고정족은 rapidity `−η`의 족 `(−√5/3,0,m_J)`, `|m_J|≤2/3`(순수 끝점만이 Thm 4.5의 `N=2` 극값 궤도)이며 후선택 분기에서만 기록을 보존한다(성공확률 `φ⁻⁴`/cycle, Thm 6.4). 실패 분기 유니터리 `V`는 `VKV†=K`이면 여전히 unital이고 최대 강도에서는 채널을 바꾸지 않는다(`V=e^{iπK/4}`); K축을 돌리는 `V`만 non-unital 완성을 준다(Remark 4.6.2, C51).

**A에서 나오는 종방향 생존 가지(Cor 5.12, v1.4 신규).** `σ_q = A^q/tr(A^q) = ½(I+tanh(qη)K)`는 `A`만의 함수이고 `Tr(σ_q J)=0`(Lemma 3.3)이지만 `m_K=tanh(qη)≠0`이다. 자원축 `w=e_K`(역시 `K=(2A−3I)/√5`로 `A`의 함수)로 읽으면 모든 허용각에서 `κ_*=−χ tanh(qη)`, 여유 `sech²(qη)`인 유일 faithful 고정점이 나오며, `σ_q`는 무조건부 return의 불변상태다(`q=2`, `χ=+1`, `α=π/4`: `M=0`, `b=(0,0,−3√5/7)`, 여유 `4/49`, 한 번의 충돌로 도달; C67). 따라서 v1.3 §6.7의 "생존 판정값은 `A`만의 함수이면 안 된다(Lemma 3.3)"는 **철회**한다(v1.3 감사 F1). 다만 frame은 `J`를 함께 쓰며, `q`·frame·`α`·`χ`·instrument·공급원을 작용이 골랐다는 증명은 없다 — **형식적 증인이지 작용 선택이 아니다.**

**남은 물리 잔여(§6.7, v1.2 재정형; v1.4에서 A-금지문 철회).** v1.1의 "남은 것은 각 `β` 하나"는 지지되지 않는다. `w_K`는 선언된 Clifford-pair 모형 안에서 **생존 판정값**이며, 작용이 아직 고정했다고 증명되지 않은 객체는 다음이다: 전체 frame `(u,v,w)`(같은 `w`에서 면내 회전의 자유가 남음: W46), `α`와 `χ`, 무조건부 초기 `m_K(0)` 또는 조건부 후선택 비용의 판독, instrument 강도 `λ`와 실패 분기 `V`, 독립 reference 공급원과 재사용 시 역작용, 판독·작용 기원. 정확한 문장: *선언된 Clifford-pair 충돌 모형에서, 수동 return 후 남는 기록의 존재 조건을 자원축의 광추 투영 `w_K`로 표현했다. 작용이 결합·instrument·기준상태를 선택하는 문제는 열려 있다.*

**M67 부채.** Obs. 38.6은 Lemma 8.1로 닫힌다(고정 `k_⊥`, 모든 `θ`, 유계 실수 profile, **점별**). Cor. 38.7은 대수 수준에서 닫힌다(`α|_∂ ∈ πℤ`, `α∈W^{1,∞}`). "모든 spectral regulator"로의 확장은 kernel 대각·적분 교환 조건을 붙인 범위로 축소한다(§8.1 scope (vii)).

**Z-Spin 고유성(v1.2 감사 §6).** filtering·dephasing 기전은 임의의 쌍곡 `A_t=e^{η_tK}`, `tr A_t=t>2`에서 성립하고 인자는 `f_t=((t−√(t²−4))/2)²`이다(C65); `t=3`이 `φ⁻⁴`를 준다. 따라서 이 논문은 **일반 qubit filtering·collision 현상의 trace-three 특수화**이며, Z-action이 그 class와 instrument를 고른다는 주장은 별개로 열려 있다.

**상태.** OUTPUT ROLE SUPPORT+METHOD, CORE 없음, SSOT 변경 없음, `qualified-human anchor: NONE`, RQ3 OPEN→OPEN, RQ2는 정량적 fibre/return 자산의 증가로만 전진. 라벨 **MANUSCRIPT DRAFT**(v1.3 감사자: 두 S2를 국소 수리한 뒤 SUPPORT 범위 동결 검토 가능, 그 다음은 작용 유도 계산). 다음 물리 계산은 `w_K`를 고르기 전에 S14에서 경계 유효 결합텐서 `G`를 유도하고 그것이 M68의 equal-strength orthogonal Clifford-pair class에 속하는지 검사하는 것이다(§6.7, 감사자 제안). 동일 계보 자기감사 한도(2/2) 도달 — 이 판본의 저자측 검토는 감사 항목의 기계 재검증(§B)이며 독립 감사가 아니다.

---

## 1. Introduction

### 1.0 Abstract

We ask whether a reversible integral holonomy `(A, J)`, `JAJ = A⁻¹`, `tr A = 3`, soldered onto the chiral-bag boundary carrier of ZS-M67, can both generate and preserve a physical record. *Gap:* the corpus had a two-species collision model and no exact statement of what the holonomy does to the reference between records. *Results:* on the reference Bloch vector each passive (post-selected) return is a Lorentz boost of rapidity `2η = 2 arcosh(3/2)` along the odd direction `K`; the canonical two-outcome completion of the post-selection (a Lüders operation with effect `e^{−2η}A²`) has an unconditional channel that is exact dephasing in the light-cone basis with factor `φ⁻⁴`, so the longitudinal population `m_K` is conserved while the transverse components `(m_{K'}, m_J)` decay exactly as `φ^{−4n}` (unconditionally) or as `m(0)cosh ζ₀/cosh(ζ₀+2nη)` (conditionally, where `m_K → 1`), bounded by `sech(ζ₀+2nη)`; the identity (cumulative success probability) × (transverse reference retention) `= e^{−2nη}` holds for every reference with nonzero transverse population; a transverse population `≥ r` cannot persist through `N` consecutive post-selected returns unless `r ≤ sech((N−1)η)` (`2/3`, `2/7`, …), sharply. A coupling through a commutative carrier algebra is unital and flip-covariant and cannot record. For the Clifford-pair couplings `X_E⊗(u·σ) + χY_E⊗(v·σ)`, `w = u×v`, the fixed point is unique for `sin 2α ≠ 0` and its record is `κ_* = −χ m_w(1−cos 2α)/(1−(1−T_w²)cos 2α)` with `m_w = m·w` (SO(3) covariance), with the non-strict bound `|κ_*| ≤ 2|m_w|/(2−T_w²) ≤ 2|m_w|/(1+m_w²) ≤ 1` (first inequality strict iff `m_w ≠ 0` and `T_w > 0`; second an equality iff `m_w = 0` or the reference is pure) and the exact faithfulness identity `1−|r^*|² = [T_w⁴ + (1−|m|²)(T_w²sin²2α + (1−cos2α)²)]/D²` (mixed iff the reference is not pure-diagonal along `w`). The joint grading `J_E⊗J` is a symmetry of the coupling iff `w = ±e_J`; every coupling with `w_K = w·e_K = 0` — a strictly larger class — has vanishing limiting record under passive return in both readings. In the conditional reading with `m_K(0) > −1` the limiting record `κ_∞ = −χ w_K(1−cos2α)/(1−w_K²cos2α)` vanishes iff `w_K = 0`, is biased and faithful iff `0 < |w_K| < 1`, and is a pure pole at `|w_K| = 1`; in the unconditional reading the limiting record `−χ w_K m_K(0)(1−cos2α)/(1−(1−m_K(0)²(1−w_K²))cos2α)` vanishes iff `w_K m_K(0) = 0` and is biased and faithful iff `0 < |w_K m_K(0)| < 1` (so `|w_K| = 1` with `0 < |m_K(0)| < 1` gives a faithful biased record without refresh). In the conditional model, for `0 < |w_K| < 1`, the supremum over the collision angle, `2|w_K|/(1+w_K²)`, is not attained (at the endpoints `|w_K| ∈ {0,1}` it is attained at every admissible angle); a target `ρ` is reached at some admissible angle iff `|w_K| > x(ρ) = (1−√(1−ρ²))/ρ` (exact, not a rounded decimal). The reversal-blindness identity `Tr f(A)J = 0` forbids only a `J`-population built from the holonomy: the `A`-derived references `A^q/tr(A^q) = ½(I + tanh(qη)K)`, which are invariant under the unconditional return, are read by a resource axis `w = e_K` with the faithful biased record `−χ tanh(qη)` and margin `sech²(qη)` at every admissible angle (Corollary 5.12) — a formal branch, with no action selection of the frame, angle, chirality, instrument or reference supply. For the soldered coupling a `T₂`-magnitude held-reference capacity survives at most two passive returns, and two are attained at a finite admissible angle (`16/19` on consecutive points of the pure orbit); **for the soldered resource-axis class `w = ±e_J`**, where the transducer coherence `T_w` is the odd coherence `T`, the joint demand `T ≥ M_*`, `|κ| = T₂` on one reference has maximal fixed-angle capacity `B(c) = √(1−M_*²)(1−c)/(1−(1−M_*²)c)` and is excluded iff `cos 2α > c_crit = −0.6369…`; outside that class the exclusion fails (`w = e_K`, `m = T₂e_K`, `α = π/4` attains `|κ_*| = T₂` faithfully). In the declared fresh-copy collision model the actual sequential process converges to these limiting records (the carrier relaxation matrix is Schur-stable for every reference), but the carrier record's lifetime depends on the collision relaxation as well as on the `φ⁻⁴` reference law (exact counterexample). No unital operation restores an unconditionally erased transverse resource, even if it varies from cycle to cycle: `(1−f²)Σ_n|P_⊥r_n|² ≤ |r_0|²`. Separately, a `k_⊥`-dressed antiunitary symmetry `𝒮_k = γ⁰e^{−iφ_kΣ³/2}𝒦`, `φ_k = 2 arg k_⊥ − π`, of the chiral-bag half-space Dirac operator forces the pointwise level-wise vanishing of the axial density and closes an observation of ZS-M67. *Limitations:* the action origin of the coupling frame, the collision angle and chirality, the reference state and its supply, the instrument strength and failure branch, and the readout are all open; the subsystem/dilation structure behind the reduced collision channel (A5) is a declared hypothesis, not a consequence of the soldering; the identification of the transfer with the physical return is a hypothesis; the dephasing form of the instrument is an imported (Lüders) structure; novelty of the coherent-reference results awaits full-text sweeps; no human review has been obtained.

### 1.1 Question, gap, contribution

ZS-M67 established that a faithful biased record on the chiral-bag boundary carrier requires a flip-odd (grading-odd) external resource and left the selector as `D-S14-EVENT-001` (OPEN), with one necessary condition (`T/Δ ≤ 0.4147`, M67 Cor. 41.1). The Z-Spin reversible holonomy `(A,J)` with `JAJ = A⁻¹`, `tr A = 3` supplies, by construction, an oriented frame on a two-dimensional reference fibre: its invariant Lorentz form `g_L` and the odd pair `(K_R, K'_R)` are exactly what a chiral coupling needs (Mission RQ3: "genuinely external, non-phase-covariant"). This paper asks one question:

> **Can a reversible-holonomy Clifford carrier both generate and preserve a physical record?**

*Gap.* Before this line, the corpus had a two-species collision model (`H`-mode odd transfer plus `B`-mode population drift) and no statement about what the holonomy itself does to the reference between records. The seed line found that the same holonomy that supplies the frame acts non-unitarily on the reference state and asked for its exact effect.

*Contribution.* Under the explicit assumptions of §2, we (i) prove the exact return law on the reference Bloch vector — each post-selected return is a Lorentz boost of rapidity `2η` along `K` (Theorems 4.1, 4.3) — and complete it canonically to a two-outcome instrument whose unconditional channel is exact K-dephasing at `φ⁻⁴` (Theorem 4.6, the reference-resource decay law; trade-off Theorem 4.7); (ii) prove that passive return depletes every transverse reference resource with the sharp `N`-return ceilings `sech((N−1)η)` (Theorems 4.4–4.5); (iii) prove that a coupling through a commutative algebra can never record (Theorem 5.1), derive the exact fixed point of the chiral coupling with its non-strict amplification bound and faithfulness identity (Theorems 5.2, 5.3, 5.7), classify the Clifford-pair coupling class by its resource axis `w` and separate the symmetry classification (`w = ±e_J`) from the survival classification (`w_K = 0`) (Theorem 5.8), prove the survival classification of the limiting record with its exact faithfulness and attainability conditions (Theorem 5.9), exhibit the `A`-derived longitudinal surviving branch that the reversal-blindness lemma does **not** exclude (Corollary 5.12), prove that the actual fresh-copy collision process reaches the limiting record (Lemma 5.10) and that the carrier record's lifetime is not the reference law (Proposition 5.11); (iv) re-type the refresh requirement as a map-level problem, prove the `χ = 2η` cancellation (Theorem 6.2), the involutive-cycle theorem (6.4), the no-unital-fixed-point theorem (6.5) and the asymptotic transverse depletion under any sequence of unital refreshes (Theorem 6.6), and re-type the residue as the survival datum `w_K` plus the list of objects the action has not been shown to fix (§6.7); (v) prove the `k_⊥`-dressed antiunitary lemma that closes M67 Observation 38.6 and the algebraic half of M67 Corollary 38.7 (§8).

One-sentence contribution (MANUSCRIPT §1): *Under the declared reversible trace-three holonomy and boundary-carrier assumptions, we prove the exact reference-state return dynamics and its canonical instrument completion, show that passive holonomy erases every transverse reference resource with sharp `N`-return ceilings while conserving the light-cone population, construct the Clifford-pair record transducer with an exact fixed point, amplification bound and faithfulness identity, and prove that the survival of its limiting record under passive return is decided by the light-cone projection `w_K` of the coupling's resource axis (conditionally zero iff `w_K = 0` and biased-and-faithful iff `0 < |w_K| < 1`; unconditionally zero iff `w_K m_K(0) = 0` and biased-and-faithful iff `0 < |w_K m_K(0)| < 1`, with `κ_∞ = −χ w_K m_K(0)` at `|w_K| = 1`; in the conditional model attained at an admissible angle iff `|w_K| > x(ρ)`), that a holonomy-derived longitudinal reference is one such surviving branch (Corollary 5.12), that the actual sequential collisions reach the limiting record, and that no unital refresh restores an erased transverse resource — changing the RQ3 residue from "a refresh map" to "the survival datum `w_K` within a declared model, with the coupling frame, collision angle, chirality, initial population, instrument, reference supply and readout still to be derived from the action", and changing the seed's claimed one-return target exclusion to a proven three-return exclusion.*

### 1.2 What this paper does not claim (non-claims)

- It does not derive the reference state, `m_K(0)`, `m_J`, the coupling frame `(u,v,w)`, the coupling angle `α`, the chirality `χ`, the instrument strength `λ`, the failure branch `V`, or any refresh map from the Z-Spin action (S14). `NC-M68.1`
- It does not identify the `h_L`-adjoint transfer with a Hamiltonian time evolution, nor `2η` with a physical rate, nor the number of returns with physical time. `NC-M68.2`
- It does not emit a no-go on the existence of an action-derived refresh map (the No-Go Emission Gate is not passed; §6.7). `NC-M68.3`
- It does not claim external novelty for any result; the closest prior art is typed in §12, and several results are `OPEN-NOVELTY` pending a broader full-text sweep. `NC-M68.4`
- It does not claim the physical Lorentz structure of spacetime from the Lorentz group of the filtering map (Mission RQ2: "rotation algebra ≠ metric dimension"). `NC-M68.5`
- It does not address a definite outcome or Born weighting (M67 S6). `NC-M68.6`
- It does not claim that the Z-Spin action supplies a resource axis with `w_K ≠ 0`, nor that it forbids one; Theorem 5.9 is a classification, not a selection. `NC-M68.7`
- It does not claim that the instrument of Theorem 4.6 is *the* physical instrument; it is one canonical completion of A3 (the Lüders completion of the effect `e^{−2η}A²`), with its strength and failure-branch freedoms declared (§10.1). `NC-M68.8`
- It does not claim that the `φ⁻⁴` law is the lifetime of a physical carrier record (Prop 5.11), nor that the limiting records of Theorem 5.9 are reached outside the fresh-copy model of A5 (Lemma 5.10 needs independent copies; back-action on a re-used reference is open). `NC-M68.9`
- It does not claim that the remaining physical residue is a single scalar; `w_K` is the survival datum of a declared model (§6.7). `NC-M68.10`
- It does not claim that the `A`-derived reference family, resource axis, angle or chirality of Corollary 5.12 are selected by the action, nor that its capacity `tanh(2η) = 3√5/7 > T₂` is a physical prediction; the corollary refutes an over-strong prohibition and is a formal witness (`NC-M68.1` applies to it verbatim). `NC-M68.11`
- It does not claim that the boundary carrier is an autonomous physical subsystem with the tensor factorisation and partial trace used in §5–§6; A5 declares it (K17). `NC-M68.12`
